📤 MBM Post Kit — every video, ready to paste

Work top to bottom — the number is the posting order. Tap a video to open it. Every box has a COPY button and says exactly where the text goes. Every video posts to all four platforms; on videos over 3:00 the card carries a heads-up about Instagram reach — you never have to figure out platform rules. Upload files live on the computer in social/exports/; thumbnails in social/thumbs/. After posting, tick the chips on the live tracker.

The Anointing at Bethany — she hath done what she could (Mark 14, KJV)
YouTube — Description YouTube Studio → Details → Description box 
Two days before the Passover, in the house of Simon the leper, a woman broke an alabaster box of ointment of spikenard, very precious, and poured it on his head. Some had indignation — it might have been sold for more than three hundred pence and given to the poor. Jesus said, Let her alone; she hath done what she could — and wheresoever this gospel is preached throughout the whole world, this that she hath done shall be spoken of for a memorial of her.

What would it look like to give what you actually have, instead of what you wish you had?

Mark 14:3–9, from the KJV.

This Gospel Shall Be Preached in All the World — then shall the end come (Matthew 24, KJV)
YouTube — Description YouTube Studio → Details → Description box 
On the mount of Olives the disciples asked him privately, What shall be the sign of thy coming, and of the end of the world? The end waits on the telling.

Do you think that promise is being fulfilled right now?

Matthew 24:3–14, from the KJV.
